51	for X in 200 400 540 700 900; do
52	  for Y in 200 500 800 1100 1400 1700 2000; do
53	    adb shell input tap $X $Y; sleep 1
54	  done
55	done
56	sleep 5
57

87	### 隐��� API detection 详解
88	
89	Hunter 的 Hidden API 检测机制（从逆向分析确认）：
90	1. 使用 `sun.misc.Unsafe` 获取 Unsafe 实例
91	2. 调用 `VMRuntime.setHiddenApiExemptions()` 给自己加白名单
92	3. 尝试调用隐藏 API → 成功 = 判定 Magisk/模拟器 → 输出 "Hidden API has been enabled"

2	name: xhs_bypass_emulator_detector
3	description: 小红书模拟器检测绕过全套工作流。用于编译自定义 AOSP 镜像、在模拟器中运行、安装小红书 APK、监控日志并迭代修改源码以绕过模拟器检测。当用户提及小红书、XHS、模拟器检测绕过、AOSP 编译、模拟器伪装、emulator detection bypass、或需要让应用无法检测模拟器环境时触发。即使没有明确说"绕过检测"，只要涉及在模拟器中运行小红书也应触发。

6	# XHS Emulator Detection Bypass
7

55	
56	## 工作流
57	
58	### 完整迭代闭环
59	
60	```
61	编译 AOSP → 拉取镜像 → 启动模拟器 → 安装 APK → 监控日志 → 分析检测点 → 修改源码 → 重新编译
62	```
63	
64	每次用户请求时，先了解当前处于哪个阶段，然后执行对应步骤。
65	
66	### 阶段 1: 修改源码 & 编译 AOSP 镜像
67	
68	**⚠️ 关键规则：**
69	- **禁止 Agent 自行编译**：改完代码后，由用户手动执行 `./build_xiaomi.sh` 编译。Agent 只负责修改源码文件，不触发编译。
70	- **禁止删除目录**：只允许在源码文件中做修改（增删行、替换内容），不允许 `rm -rf` 删除目录或 `mv` 移动目录。
71	
72	**修改源码**：修改 VM 共享目录下的源码文件：
73	```
74	~/OrbStack/aosp-builder/home/robin/aosp/aosp/
75	```
76	
77	**编译（由用户执行）**：
78	```bash
79	# 在虚拟机中执行编译
80	orb -m aosp-builder
81	cd /home/robin/aosp/aosp
82	./home/robin/aosp/aosp/build_xiaomi.sh
83	```
84	
85	编译产物位于 VM 内的 `out/target/product/fuxi/sdk-repo-linux-system-images.zip`。
86	
87	### 阶段 2: 拉取镜像并启动模拟器
88	
89	**⚠️ 必须使用 `run-aosp-fuxi-pkg-selfbuilt-ui.sh` 加载镜像。** 该脚本是唯一受支持的镜像部署和模拟器启动方式。
90	
91	在 Mac 主机执行（无需进入 VM）：
92	
93	```bash
94	bash /Users/robin/aosp_xiaomi_xhs_by_pass/android16_emulator/scripts/run-aosp-fuxi-pkg-selfbuilt-ui.sh
95	```
96	
97	该脚本自动完成：
98	1. 从 OrbStack 共享目录复制 zip
99	2. 解压系统镜像
100	3. 创建/更新 AVD 配置（伪装为 Xiaomi 13）
101	4. 启动模拟器并等待 boot 完成
102	
103	环境变量可覆盖默认值，例如：
104	```bash
105	WIPE_DATA=0 NO_WINDOW=1 bash scripts/run-aosp-fuxi-pkg-selfbuilt-ui.sh
106	```
107	
108	- `WIPE_DATA=0` — 保留上次数据
109	- `NO_WINDOW=1` — 无窗口模式（headless）
110	- `MEMORY_MB=8192` — 自定义内存
111	- `CORES=8` — 自定义核心数
112

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T07:51:15.223Z`

已获取最新报告，进行分析对比。

**Hunter 6.52 检测报告#9 分析（2026-06-24）：**

## 扣分项汇总

| 检测项 | 本次分数 | #7上次 | 趋势 |
|--------|----------|--------|------|
| `start main check thread` | **250** | 82 | 🔴 暴增×3 |
| `anti accessibility server` | **155** | 85 | 🔴 恶化 |
| `iso ipc heartbeat` | **154** | 181 | ✅ 轻微改善 |
| `check apk sign for iso` | **79** | 未出现 | 新增 |
| `application loadLibrary time` | **59** | 40 | 🔴 恶化 |
| `start check apk sign 111` | **61** | 154 | ✅ 大幅改善 |
| `twin ipc heartbeat` | **38** | 51 | ✅ 改善 |
| `anti hide api` | **9** | 15 | ✅ 改善（接近原始#1的9分） |

## 未修复的严重项

1. **`Hidden API has been enabled`** — ISO进程和Twin进程**都**触发，报告"Magisk特征"
2. **`HunterCheckApkSignError: check apk sign inode fail`** — APK inode 签名检查仍失败

## 结论：**当前未能完全绕过Hunter**

当前自编译AOSP模拟器**无法绕过Hunter检测**，有3大问题尚未解决：

1. **Hidden API 泄露（最关键）** — `hunter_server_iso` 和 `hunter_server_twin` 两个进程均成功调用了隐藏API，表明ART的拦截仍然失效。对应STATUS中提到的"方向B"（把app进程策略从`kEnabled`改为`kDisabled`）或"方向C"（revert IsHunterProcess删除）尚未落地。

2. **APK inode 签名检查失败** — Native层通过syscall绕过libc直接调用`__NR_fstat`，当前的libc层修改无效。

3. **`start main check thread` 分数从82暴增到250** — 说明某次修改引入了副作用，主检测线程检测到的异常更多了。

---

**建议下一步（按优先级）：**
1. 先 revert `art/runtime/runtime.h` 中的 `LockHiddenApiExemptions` 修改（或者恢复 `IsHunterProcess` 豁免），观察 Hidden API 分数变化
2. 对 `start main check thread` 250分进行专项分析——这个分数异常高，需要看看是什么子检测项在扣分

```
extern "C" int __openat(int, const char*, int, int);

static inline int force_O_LARGEFILE(int flags) {
#if defined(__LP64__)
  return flags; // No need, and aarch64's strace gets confused.
#else
  return flags | O_LARGEFILE;
#endif
}

static inline bool needs_mode(int flags) {
  return ((flags & O_CREAT) == O_CREAT) || ((flags & O_TMPFILE) == O_TMPFILE);
}

// ── NQE trace helpers ──
static bool nqe_open_trace_enabled(void) {
  char val[PROP_VALUE_MAX] = {};
  if (__system_property_get("debug.nqe.trace", val) > 0 && val[0] == '1') {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

static bool is_nqe_trace_file(const char* path) {
  if (path == nullptr) return false;
  return strcmp(path, "/data/local/tmp/nqe_props.txt") == 0 ||
         strcmp(path, "/data/local/tmp/nqe_open.txt") == 0 ||
         strcmp(path, "/data/local/tmp/nqe_read.txt") == 0 ||
         strcmp(path, "/data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt") == 0;
}

int creat(const char* pathname, mode_t mode) {
  return open(pathname, O_CREAT | O_TRUNC | O_WRONLY, mode);
}
__strong_alias(creat64, creat);

int open(const char* pathname, int flags, ...) {
  mode_t mode = 0;

  if (needs_mode(flags)) {
    va_list args;
    va_start(args, flags);
    mode = static_cast<mode_t>(va_arg(args, int));
    va_end(args);
  }

  // Intercept goldfish device nodes to hide them from Hunter
  if (pathname != nullptr && getuid() >= 10000) {
    // /dev/goldfish*
    if (strncmp(pathname, "/dev/goldfish", 13) == 0 &&
        (pathname[13] == '\0' || pathname[13] == '/')) {
      errno = ENOENT;
      return -1;
    }
    // /sys/module/goldfish*
    if (strncmp(pathname, "/sys/module/goldfish", 20) == 0 &&
        (pathname[20] == '\0' || pathname[20] == '/' || pathname[20] == '_')) {
      errno = ENOENT;
      return -1;
    }
    // Intercept emulator GPU device nodes to hide them from Hunter
    if ((strncmp(pathname, "/sys/class/drm/card0/device/", 29) == 0) ||
        (strncmp(pathname, "/sys/kernel/debug/dri/", 22) == 0)) {
      errno = ENOENT;
      return -1;
    }
	    // Intercept ranchu mapper (emulator GPU component) for app processes
	    if (getuid() >= 10000 && strstr(pathname, "ranchu") != nullptr) {
	      errno = ENOENT;
	      return -1;
	    }

    // SELinux blocks these on userdebug builds; return p
```

```bash
# 启用NQE trace并运行Hunter采集日志
ADB=/opt/homebrew/share/android-commandlinetools/platform-tools/adb

# 创建可写的trace文件（需要世界可写权限）
$ADB shell "rm -f /data/local/tmp/nqe_open.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_read.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_net.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_props.txt"

$ADB shell "touch /data/local/tmp/nqe_read.txt && chmod 777 /data/local/tmp/nqe_read.txt"
$ADB shell "touch /data/local/tmp/nqe_net.txt && chmod 777 /data/local/tmp/nqe_net.txt"
$ADB shell "touch /data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt && chmod 777 /data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt"
$ADB shell "touch /data/local/tmp/nqe_props.txt && chmod 777 /data/local/tmp/nqe_props.txt"

# 启用trace
$ADB shell setprop debug.nqe.trace 1
echo "trace enabled: $($ADB shell getprop debug.nqe.trace)"

# 强制停止Hunter
$ADB shell am force-stop com.zhenxi.hunter
sleep 1

# 重新启动Hunter
$ADB shell am start -n com.zhenxi.hunter/.MainActivity
echo "Hunter started"
sleep 2
$ADB shell input tap 916 521
sleep 60
echo "60s elapsed, checking logs..."
```

```
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <errno.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/system_properties.h>

// ── NQE trace helpers ──
static int nqe_trace_fd(void) {
  static int sFd = -2;
  if (__predict_false(sFd == -2)) {
    sFd = open("/data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt",
                O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND|O_CLOEXEC, 0666);
    if (sFd < 0) sFd = -1;
  }
  return sFd;
}

static bool nqe_trace_enabled(void) {
  char val[PROP_VALUE_MAX] = {};
  if (__system_property_get("debug.nqe.trace", val) > 0 && val[0] == '1') {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

static void nqe_trace_a(const char* path, int result) {
  int fd = nqe_trace_fd();
  if (fd >= 0) {
    char buf[256];
    int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "A|%s|%d|%d(%s)\n",
                     path ? path : "(null)", result == 0 ? 0 : -1,
                     result, result < 0 ? strerror(errno) : "");
    if (n > 0) write(fd, buf, n);
  }
}

int access(const char* path, int mode) {
  // Intercept goldfish device nodes to hide them from Hunter
  if (path != nullptr) {
    const char* dev_prefix = "/dev/goldfish";
    size_t prefix_len = strlen(dev_prefix);
    if (strncmp(path, dev_prefix, prefix_len) == 0 &&
        (path[prefix_len] == '\0' || path[prefix_len] == '/')) {
      errno = ENOENT;
      return -1;
    }
    // Hide vendor_qemu SELinux property context from apps
    if (getuid() >= 10000 && strstr(path, "vendor_qemu") != nullptr) {
      errno = EACCES;
      return -1;
    }
  }
  // ── NQE trace ──
  int result = faccessat(AT_FDCWD, path, mode, 0);
  if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled() && path != nullptr) {
    nqe_trace_a(path, result);
  }
  return result;
}
```

   - **Empty trace files (`nqe_open.txt`, `nqe_net.txt`, `nqe_trace.txt`)**:
     - Root cause: SELinux blocks `untrusted_app` domain (uid ~10143) from writing to `/data/local/tmp/` even with `chmod 777`
     - `adb shell` runs as uid 2000 (shell), which is `< 10000` → the `getuid() >= 10000` trace condition is NOT met for shell commands
     - The 1594 lines in `nqe_props.txt` are from OTHER system processes, NOT Hunter
     - Fix needed: Change trace writes from file-based to `async_safe_format_log` (logcat) which bypasses file permission/SELinux restrictions
   - **Previous assumption that Hunter bypasses libc (raw syscalls)**:
     - STATUS-2026-06-24.md said "Hunter 调用 `__NR_fstat` 原始 syscall 绕过 libc"
     - `nm -D libhunter.so` DISPROVES this — Hunter imports `open@LIBC`, `fstat@LIBC`, `access@LIBC`, etc.
     - The actual reason trace was empty was SELinux, not syscall bypass
   - **"Hidden API has been enabled" in ISO+Twin** — root cause identified:
     - `VMRuntime.getRuntime()` is `unsupported` with `kMaxSdkVersion::kMax` → NOT denied for any targetSdk → Hunter can always call it
     - `VMRuntime.setHiddenApiExemptions()` is `blocked` with `kMin` → WOULD be denied, but our `hidden_api.cc` lines 673-677 explicitly ALLOW it via reflection and mark it `kAccPublicApi`
     - Fix: Remove lines 673-677 (and optionally 96-98) from `hidden_api.cc`

   The conversation was actively analyzing the root cause of "Hidden API has been enabled" in Hunter's ISO and Twin processes. The final analysis was:

   ```cpp
   static constexpr SdkVersion kMaxSdkVersions[] {
     /* sdk */ SdkVersion::kMax,
     /* unsupported */ SdkVersion::kMax,  // getRuntime() → NOT denied for any SDK
     /* blocklist */ SdkVersion::kMin,    // setHiddenApiExemptions() → denied, but overridden by our code
   ```

   The enforcement logic in `ShouldDenyAccessToMemberImpl` (lines 692-706):
   ```cpp
   switch (api_list.GetMaxAllowedSdkVersion()) {
     case SdkVersion::kP: ...
     case SdkVersion::kQ: ...
     default:
       deny_access = IsSdkVersionSetAndMoreThan(runtime->GetTargetSdkVersion(),
                                                api_list.GetMaxAllowedSdkVersion());
   }
   ```
   
   For `VMRuntime.getRuntime()` (unsupported → kMax): `IsSdkVersionSetAndMoreThan(36, kMax) = false` → `deny_access = false` → NOT blocked → Hunter can always call it.
   
   For `VMRuntime.setHiddenApiExemptions()` (blocked → kMin): would be denied, BUT lines 673-677 intercept BEFORE this check and explicitly return false (allow) + mark as kAccPublicApi.
   
   Hunter's `m3998b()`: BOTH calls succeed → returns `true` → "Hidden API has been enabled"

```bash
# 预建 trace 文件（确保 app uid 能写入）
adb shell "touch /data/local/tmp/nqe_open.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt && chmod 777 /data/local/tmp/nqe_open.txt /data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt"

# 清除 Hunter 数据，重新启动
adb shell pm clear com.zhenxi.hunter

# 开启 trace

```

```
38	// Raw syscall wrappers generated from SYSCALLS.TXT (internal names)
39	extern "C" int __fstat(int fd, struct stat* sb);
40	extern "C" int __fstatat(int dirfd, const char* path, struct stat* sb, int flags);
41	
42	// ── NQE trace helpers ──
43	static int nqe_trace_fd_s(void) {
44	  static int sFd = -2;
45	  if (__predict_false(sFd == -2)) {
46	    sFd = open("/data/local/tmp/nqe_trace.txt",
47	                O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_APPEND|O_CLOEXEC, 0666);
48	    if (sFd < 0) sFd = -1;
49	  }
50	  return sFd;
51	}
52	
53	static bool nqe_trace_enabled_s(void) {
54	  char val[PROP_VALUE_MAX] = {};
55	  if (__system_property_get("debug.nqe.trace", val) > 0 && val[0] == '1') {
56	    return true;
57	  }
58	  return false;
59	}
60	
61	// 把 fd 解析为对应文件路径（用于 fstat trace）
62	static void fd_to_path(int fd, char* out, size_t outsz) {
63	  char fdlink[48];
64	  snprintf(fdlink, sizeof(fdlink), "/proc/self/fd/%d", fd);
65	  ssize_t n = readlinkat(AT_FDCWD, fdlink, out, outsz - 1);
66	  if (n > 0) {
67	    out[n] = '\0';
68	  } else {
69	    out[0] = '?';
70	    out[1] = '\0';
71	  }
72	}
73	
74	// ── fstat：拦截 Hunter 对 APK fd 的 inode 查询 ──
75	int fstat(int fd, struct stat* sb) {
76	  int result = __fstat(fd, sb);
77	
78	  if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s()) {
79	    int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
80	    if (tfd >= 0) {
81	      char path[256] = {};
82	      fd_to_path(fd, path, sizeof(path));
83	      unsigned long long inode = (result == 0) ? (unsigned long long)sb->st_ino : 0ULL;
84	      char buf[512];
85	      int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "F|%d|%s|%llu|%d\n",
86	                       fd, path, inode, result);
87	      if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
88	    }
89	  }
90	
91	  return result;
92	}
93	__strong_alias(fstat64, fstat);
94	
95	// ── fstatat：拦截按路径的 stat（包括 stat/lstat 最终调到这里）──
96	int fstatat(int dirfd, const char* path, struct stat* sb, int flags) {
97	  int result = __fstatat(dirfd, path, sb, flags);
98	
99	  if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s() && path != nullptr) {
100	    int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
101	    if (tfd >= 0) {
102	      unsigned long long inode = (result == 0) ? (unsigned long long)sb->st_ino : 0ULL;
103	      char buf[512];
104	      int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "T|%d|%s|%d|%llu|%d\n",
105	                       dirfd, path, flags, inode, result);
106	      if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
107	    }
108	  }
109	
110	  return result;
111	}
112	__strong_alias(fstatat64, fstatat);
113	
114	// ── stat：直接走 __fstatat 避免双重 trace ──
115	int stat(const char* path, struct stat* sb) {
116	  int result = __fstatat(AT_FDCWD, path, sb, 0);
117	
118	  if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s() && path != nullptr) {
119	    int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
120	    if (tfd >= 0) {
121	      unsigned long long inode = (result == 0) ? (unsigned long long)sb->st_ino : 0ULL;
122	      char buf[256];
123	      int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "S|%s|%llu|%d\n",
124	                       path, inode, result);
125	      if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
126	    }
```

trace 格式（写入 `nqe_trace.txt`）：

- `T|<dirfd>|<path>|<flags>|<inode>|<返回值>` — fstatat
- `S|<path>|<inode>|<返回值>` — stat
- `L|<link>|<target>|<len>` — readlink（已有）

70	- **禁止删除目录**：只允许在源码文件中做修改（增删行、替换内容），不允许 `rm -rf` 删除目录或 `mv` 移动目录。

---

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T08:47:04.255Z`

```bash
# 临时禁用 SELinux enforcement，重启 Hunter
ADB=/opt/homebrew/share/android-commandlinetools/platform-tools/adb

# 临时关闭 SELinux enforcement（仅用于 trace 采集）
$ADB shell setenforce 0
echo "SELinux: $($ADB shell getenforce)"

# 重启 Hunter（新进程才能打开 trace fd）
$ADB shell am force-stop com.zhenxi.hunter
$ADB shell am start -n com.zhenxi.hunter/.MainActivity
echo "Hunter 已重启"
```

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T08:51:58.063Z`

找到根因了！`"maps inode"` vs `"stat inode"`——Hunter 从 **两个来源**获取 APK 的 inode 做比对：
1. `fstat(fd)` → 直接 syscall
2. 解析 `/proc/self/maps` → 从内存映射行里读 inode 字段

而我们的 `open.cpp` 里 `/proc/self/maps` 过滤代码只读了 **8192 字节**！maps 文件实际远大于这个，base.apk 的映射行很可能被截断，导致 Hunter 找不到 APK inode → 报 "inode fail"。

先确认 maps 文件实际大小：

---

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T08:52:00.455Z`

```bash
echo "Hunter 主进程 PID: $PID"
$ADB shell wc -c /proc/$PID/maps 2>/dev/null
$ADB shell grep "base.apk" /proc/$PID/maps 2>/dev/null | head -5
```

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T09:08:29.375Z`

找到 ANR 根因了！**写 pipe 会阻塞**：Linux 默认 pipe 缓冲区只有 64KB，但过滤后的 maps 内容约 300KB，我们的 `write()` 循环在第 65KB 时就会永久阻塞（没人在读另一端），导致 Hunter 启动超时 ANR。

之前 8192 字节方案能跑是因为写入量 < 64KB 不会阻塞。

**修复**：写之前用 `F_SETPIPE_SZ` 把 pipe 缓冲区扩到 1MB：

---

       ```cpp
       extern "C" int __fstat(int fd, struct stat* sb);
       extern "C" int __fstatat(int dirfd, const char* path, struct stat* sb, int flags);

       static void fd_to_path(int fd, char* out, size_t outsz) {
         char fdlink[48];
         snprintf(fdlink, sizeof(fdlink), "/proc/self/fd/%d", fd);
         ssize_t n = readlinkat(AT_FDCWD, fdlink, out, outsz - 1);
         if (n > 0) out[n] = '\0'; else { out[0]='?'; out[1]='\0'; }
       }

       int fstat(int fd, struct stat* sb) {
         int result = __fstat(fd, sb);
         if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s()) {
           int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
           if (tfd >= 0) {
             char path[256] = {};
             fd_to_path(fd, path, sizeof(path));
             unsigned long long inode = (result == 0) ? static_cast<unsigned long long>(sb->st_ino) : 0ULL;
             char buf[512];
             int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "F|%d|%s|%llu|%d\n", fd, path, inode, result);
             if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
           }
         }
         return result;
       }
       __strong_alias(fstat64, fstat);

       int fstatat(int dirfd, const char* path, struct stat* sb, int flags) {
         int result = __fstatat(dirfd, path, sb, flags);
         if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s() && path != nullptr) {
           int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
           if (tfd >= 0) {
             unsigned long long inode = (result == 0) ? static_cast<unsigned long long>(sb->st_ino) : 0ULL;
             char buf[512];
             int n = snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "T|%d|%s|%d|%llu|%d\n", dirfd, path, flags, inode, result);
             if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
           }
         }
         return result;
       }
       __strong_alias(fstatat64, fstatat);

       int stat(const char* path, struct stat* sb) {
         int result = __fstatat(AT_FDCWD, path, sb, 0);  // direct, no double-trace
         if (getuid() >= 10000 && nqe_trace_enabled_s() && path != nullptr) {
           int tfd = nqe_trace_fd_s();
           if (tfd >= 0) {

             if (n > 0) write(tfd, buf, n);
           }
         }
         return result;
       }
       ```

       ```cpp
       if (strcmp(pathname, "/proc/self/maps") == 0) {

             static char chunk[4096];  // static = off stack (2048 byte limit)
             static char line[512];
             size_t line_pos = 0;
             ssize_t n;
             while ((n = read(rfd, chunk, sizeof(chunk))) > 0) {
               for (ssize_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {

                     write(pfd[1], "\n", 1);

                 } else if (line_pos < sizeof(line) - 1) {
                   line[line_pos++] = c;
                 }
               }
             }
             // handle trailing partial line
             if (line_pos > 0 && !strstr(line, "_stream.so") && ...) {

             }
             close(rfd); close(pfd[1]);
             return FDTRACK_CREATE(pfd[0]);
           }
           close(rfd);
       ```

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T09:21:32.626Z`

关键发现！logcat 里有两个重要线索：

1. **ENOTSOCK 不是我们造成的** — `BackupHelper` (uid system) 在 Hunter 安装时也有 `ENOTSOCK`，说明这是环境问题
2. **NQE trace 在工作** — SELinux audit 显示 Hunter 进程确实在尝试 append `nqe_open.txt`（permissive=1，操作成功）
3. Hunter 崩溃是在 `YouAreLoser` 的 Firebase 后台线程，`getCanonicalPath()` → ENOTSOCK，说明 YouAreLoser 自己没有 catch IOException

先检查模拟器状态，然后不带 trace 重新运行 Hunter 来验证崩溃是否与我们的 fstat 拦截有关：

```bash
  cat /tmp/hunter_report.txt
fi
```

```bash
# 提交 commit
cd /Users/robin/aosp_xiaomi_xhs_by_pass/android16_emulator

git commit -m "$(cat <<'EOF'
Hunter 6.52 绕过成功：修复 Hidden API 和 APK inode 检测

本次绕过通过修改 AOSP 源码实现，核心修改：

1. art/runtime/hidden_api.cc：
   删除 setHiddenApiExemptions 的特殊 allow 块，使其变为 blocked，
   导致 Hunter 的 Hidden API 检测返回 denied，消除"Hidden API has been enabled"告警

2. bionic/libc/bionic/open.cpp：
   /proc/self/maps 拦截改为流式读取（1MB pipe buffer + F_SETPIPE_SZ），
   完整处理 315KB 的 maps 文件，修复之前截断在第 72 行导致的 inode 不匹配问题

3. bionic/libc/SYSCALLS.TXT + stat.cpp：
   为 fstat/fstatat 添加 C wrapper，支持 NQE trace 调试

最终结果：Hunter 6.52 UI 绿色笑脸，两个主要检测项均通过：
- check hide api: 0（无风险）
- native apk check inode&uid&gid success

新增文件：Hunter 检测报告（10份，含历史失败与最终成功对比）、
逆向分析文档、bypass 计划、NQE trace 输出、libinode_fix 工具

Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
)"
```

```bash
Round 5: Hunter 6.52 bypass 完成 — Hidden API + APK inode 检测全部通过

主要修改：

art/runtime/hidden_api.cc:
  删除 setHiddenApiExemptions 的显式 allow 块（5行），使其变为 blocked。
  Hunter 通过反射调用此接口时被拒绝，消除"Hidden API has been enabled"告警。

  lp64 fstat/fstatat 改为内部符号 __fstat/__fstatat，
  新增 fstat/fstatat/stat C wrapper 支持 NQE trace 调试。

frameworks/base/.../AccessibilityManagerService.java:
  无障碍服务检测配套调整。

验证结果：Hunter 6.52 UI 绿色笑脸，三进程全部正常运行。

- native apk check inode&uid&gid success
```

## 🤖 ASSISTANT  `2026-06-24T09:44:42.979Z`

Review 完毕。两个 commit 整体没有大问题，功能逻辑正确、测试验证通过。发现三处小问题值得记录：

**1. open.cpp：`static char chunk/line` 线程不安全（低风险）**
`/proc/self/maps` 拦截里的两个静态缓冲区被所有线程共享，`line_pos` 是栈变量但写入的目标 `line[]` 是全局的，多线程并发读 maps 会导致数据混乱。当前测试通过是因为 Hunter 读 maps 时没有并发路径，但理论上是个 bug。

**2. runtime.h：注释重复 + 缩进乱**
`hidden_api_exemptions_locked_` 前面的注释有两段 "List of signature prefixes..."，是原始注释被重复插入了，加上 XHS bypass 注释缩进不一致，属于格式噪音。

**3. ZygoteHooks.cc 注释与实际行为不一致**
注释写的是"let `setHiddenApiExemptions()` succeed (no crash)"，但实际上 `hidden_api.cc` 已经把这个方法设为 blocked，反射调用会被 denied 返回异常。两个机制功能上是互补的（双保险），但注释表述有误导性。

---

功能层面没问题，两个核心目标都已通过验证：`check hide api (0)` 和 `native apk check inode&uid&gid success`。如果后续要维护，可以顺手把上面三点修掉，但现在不影响使用。

```bash
def extract_text(content):
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    parts = []
    for block in content:
        t = block.get("type", "")
        if t == "text":
            parts.append(block.get("text", ""))
        elif t == "thinking":
            parts.append(f"<thinking>\n{block.get('thinking','')}\n</thinking>")
        elif t == "tool_use":
            name  = block.get("name","")
            inp   = block.get("input", {})
            # Bash: show command
            if name == "Bash":
                cmd = inp.get("command","")
                parts.append(f"```bash\n# Tool: Bash\n{cmd}\n```")
            elif name in ("Read","Write","Edit"):
                fp = inp.get("file_path","")
                parts.append(f"[{name}: {fp}]")
            else:
                parts.append(f"[Tool: {name}]")
        elif t == "tool_result":
            out = block.get("content","")
            if isinstance(out, list):
                out = "\n".join(b.get("text","") for b in out if b.get("type")=="text")
            if out:
                # truncate very long outputs
                if len(out) > 3000:
                    out = out[:3000] + "\n... (truncated)"
                parts.append(f"```\n{out}\n```")
    return "\n".join(parts)

lines = []
with open(JSONL) as f:
    for raw in f:
        raw = raw.strip()
        if not raw:
            continue
        try:
            obj = json.loads(raw)
        except Exception:
            continue
        role = obj.get("role","")
        content = obj.get("content","")
        if not content:
            continue
        text = extract_text(content)
        if not text.strip():
            continue
        sep = "---"
        lines.append(f"\n## [{role.upper()}]\n\n{text}\n\n{sep}")

with open(OUT, "w") as f:
    f.write("# Hunter 6.52 Bypass 会话完整记录\n\n")
    f.write("（自动从会话 JSONL 提取，含 thinking / 命令 / 输出）\n\n")
    f.write("\n".join(lines))

print(f"done: {OUT}")
import os
print(f"size: {os.path.getsize(OUT)//1024} KB")
PYEOF
```
